Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the mol_bio structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 914. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 254 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the mol_bio decomposition. By minimality, the element a^914 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 254-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
